# Skills · B · part 34

866 entries, benchmark-from-link to benefits-check. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **benchmark-from-link**: Turn a link (a research paper, a Hugging Face model, or a GitHub recipe/engine repo) plus target hardware into a benchmarked subject. It researches how to run the model, works out the quant, engine and hardware with the user, creates or reuses the inference-engines recipe, writes the subject records here, runs the tiered benchmarks, and stores the results. Use when the user shares a paper, HF or GitHub link and asks to benchmark, evaluate, run or "add" that model, or says which hardware to run it on. ([iammrduncan/inference-benchmarks](https://github.com/iammrduncan/inference-benchmarks/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/benchmark-from-link) · ★39)
- **benchmark-frontier-archaeology**: Analyze an optimization benchmark's public submissions and code history. Use to explain a new SOTA, identify the mechanism behind a score change, or reconstruct a Pareto frontier including rejected submissions. Inspect submission notes and parent-relative diffs, walk adjacent winners, verify the active implementation, distinguish measured results from projections, and identify reusable mechanisms and the next bottleneck. For implementing circuit changes, use peak-qubit-reduction or toffoli-reduction. ([jieyilong/ecdsafail_skills](https://github.com/jieyilong/ecdsafail_skills/tree/HEAD/benchmark-frontier-archaeology) · ★4)
- **benchmark-functions**: Measure function performance and compare implementations. Use when optimizing critical code paths.
